**Deduplication testing** (`tests/test_dedup.py`) is direct (non-fuzz) unit tests against small hand-built Bundles (mirroring every other pillar's own "don't reuse generator output for rule-specific tests" discipline) covering: identifier-based merge, name-fallback merge for all four resource types (`Practitioner`/`Organization`/`Location`, `Patient` implicitly covered by the same code path), no-merge on differing identifiers, no-merge when neither identifier nor name resolves, `("identifier", ...)`/`("name", ...)` key-tagging never letting an identifier-matched pair collide with a merely name-matched one, `Condition` (a representative clinical/event type) never deduplicated even with identical fields, cross-reference rewriting, the original Bundle never mutated, multiple independent duplicate groups merged correctly in one pass, and survivor entry order preserved. `test_real_837p_fixture_merges_billing_and_rendering_provider` runs the real motivating case end-to-end through the actual `convert_to_bundle()` pipeline (not a hand-built Bundle) against the existing `edi_837p_basic.x12` fixture, confirming both the practitioner count drops from two to one and `Claim.provider`/`Claim.careTeam[].provider` both correctly resolve to the kept resource. `tests/test_routes.py` covers the wiring: `deduplicate` omitted or `false` never adds a `deduplication` key to the JSON response (proving the feature is genuinely opt-in, not just default-off), `deduplicate: true` against a fixture with nothing to merge reports `resources_merged: 0` (not an error), the same 837P fixture merges through the full HTTP API, and the no-JS `/convert` form path (checkbox present as `"on"`) renders the summary line server-side. A full sweep of every registered `(message_type, trigger_event)` combination's generator output, run through `convert_to_bundle()` then `deduplicate_bundle()`, confirms the generic reference-rewriting walk never crashes against any resource shape this app produces - not committed as a standing test (it would just re-run every other pillar's own generator fuzz coverage a second time for no additional signal, since dedup's own crash surface is the generic walk, not per-mapper field shapes), but run manually before shipping this feature.

**Bidirectional-transformation testing** (`tests/test_transform.py`) covers the registry (targets are listed; an unregistered one raises `MappingError`; `get_builder` normalizes case/whitespace like every sibling registry) and, per target, a round trip through the *real* forward pipeline rather than a "text starts with MSH" assertion.

The shape is the same for every family, so it is stated once here rather than per target:
  - A field-by-field comparison against that format's own real fixture — every field the builder populates, checked against its exact expected value.
  - A genuine round trip: feed the generated text back through the real converter and assert the resulting Bundle matches.
  - `MappingError` cases for whatever the builder structurally requires (no Patient, no Appointment, no Claim).
  - A per-family fuzz sweep (200-300 seeds) of `generate(...)` output run reverse-then-forward, expected to be clean.
  - `test_reverse_transform_preserves_resource_type_counts`, parametrized across all 39 targets, asserting the resource-type multiset survives. This is the one check no per-slice test makes — they all assert on named fields of resources they assume exist, so none notices a resource vanishing. It caught a real Specimen-loss bug the whole suite was green through.

Cases worth knowing about individually:
  - **ADT**: `test_adt_a03_missing_discharge_time_raises_mapping_error` mirrors the forward mapper's own requirement; A08 is parametrized over both the finished and in-progress fixtures, proving the "no A08-specific reverse code needed" claim; the three cancel triggers assert `status="entered-in-error"` and that `period.start` survives, proving EVN-2 is genuinely inert on the way back out.
  - **SIU**: `test_siu_s26_missing_start_time_raises_mapping_error` is the one real per-trigger difference (the booked/no-show family needs a resolvable start).
  - **ORU**: asserts each report's regenerated `OBX` segments resolve back to only its own `Observation`s — the grouping guarantee, not just field values.
  - **MDM**: `test_mdm_t02_same_author_authenticator_dedups_practitioner_on_round_trip` — one Practitioner referenced by both `author` and `authenticator` must stay one.
  - **C-CDA**: `test_ccd_round_trip_preserves_coding_system_not_just_code` is the regression test for a real bug where a second cycle corrupted `system` into `urn:oid:http://snomed.info/sct`. Others cover the BP/pulse-ox panels, IVL_PQ bounds, Specimen at both attachment levels, the PractitionerRole performer chain, and `originalText` surviving two cycles.
  - **EDI**: `test_edi_278_request_with_dependent_round_trip_preserves_both_patients` regresses an `include_id=False` copied unexamined from 270; 835 asserts a non-canonical `"XV"` qualifier round-trips rather than falling back; 837I asserts `item.diagnosisSequence` stays `None`, confirming the builder never fabricates a composite `SV2` lacks.

`tests/test_routes.py` covers `/api/transform`'s error matrix (invalid JSON → 400, invalid Bundle → 422, missing Patient → 422, unregistered target → 422), the no-JS form fallback, every target appearing in the dropdown, and `test_convert_then_transform_round_trips_end_to_end` through the real HTTP layer. **UI behavior is not covered by any of these** — none of them render `app/static/app.js` — so anything UI-affecting needs a real browser (see the Puppeteer note in this project's own contributing guidance).


**Data Specification testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder.py`, `tests/test_data_specification_routes.py`) mirrors the same discipline as every other pillar's own first slice. `test_provenance_recording_does_not_change_bundle_output` is the load-bearing one - parametrized across a representative fixture for all 9 ADT triggers (both branches for A08 and A13, the two triggers whose own docstrings disclose a real behavioral split) - patches `uuid.uuid4` to an identical deterministic sequence for an untraced and a traced call to the same mapper, then asserts `bundle.model_dump(exclude_none=True)` is byte-identical between the two: instrumentation adding a `.record()`/`.record_inferred()` call must never change what a mapper actually builds, and this is the one test that would catch it if it ever did. Direct content-correctness tests check exact expected crosswalk entries against `adt_a01_basic.hl7`'s own already-known values (the same fixture `tests/test_adt_a01_mapping.py` itself asserts against, so the two test files can never silently drift apart about what this fixture actually contains), plus three dedicated regression tests for the real complications the mapper-layer instrumentation bullet above discloses: `test_adt_a02_transfer_reindexes_location_provenance_after_insert` (both `location[0]`/`location[1]` facts point at their correct, post-insert source fields, with no stray third fact left over), `test_adt_a11_cancel_admit_never_leaks_evn2_period_start_into_crosswalk` (the exact same EVN-2-mislabeling hazard `test_adt_a11_mapping.py::test_cancel_admit_without_pv1_44_does_not_leak_evn2_as_period_start` already guards against on the Bundle side, checked here against the crosswalk's own facts instead), and `test_adt_a13_with_discharge_period_start_points_at_pv1_44_not_evn2` (confirms the corrected fact, not the stale pre-correction one, survives into the resolved crosswalk). `resolve_bundle_paths`/`ProvenanceRecorder` get direct unit tests in isolation: the `bundle.id`-special-case (no `entry[N].resource.` prefix), the defensive skip-not-crash path for a `resource_id` that never made it into the final Bundle, and `record()`'s own last-write-wins semantics (`forget()`/`forget_prefix()` included) - all four exercised against small hand-built `Bundle`/`ProvenanceRecorder` objects, not real fixtures, the same "don't reuse generator/fixture output for helper-specific unit tests" discipline `test_dedup.py` already established. `tests/test_data_specification_routes.py` covers the route/API layer the same way `test_routes.py` covers every other feature: the page renders with a working nav link back to `/`, `/` itself gained a nav link forward to `/data-specification`, `/api/data-specification` against the real `adt_a01_basic.hl7` fixture returns a fully-supported report with spot-checked entries (including confirming a `derivation="inferred"` entry has no `source_location` key at all once serialized with `exclude_none=True`, not a `null` one), the real EDI fixture (`edi_270_basic.x12`) converts successfully but reports `unsupported=True` with an empty `entries` list (X12's own `unsupported=True` example - no instrumentation exists for that format at all yet), the real C-CDA fixture (`ccd_basic.xml`) converts successfully and reports `unsupported=True` too but with a genuinely non-empty `entries` list once the header/Problems instrumentation shipped (see the mapper-layer bullet above for why no CDA document type is ever added to a "fully supported" set) - plus dedicated `test_api_data_specification_siu_type_is_now_instrumented`/`test_api_data_specification_oru_type_is_now_instrumented`/`test_api_data_specification_mdm_type_is_now_instrumented` regressions proving SIU/ORU/MDM all stay reported as supported (the file's own dedicated "non-instrumented HL7v2 type" regression test, previously re-pointed at each newly-un-instrumented type in turn as SIU/ORU/MDM each shipped, was retired once no such HL7v2 example remained to point it at - its job is now fully covered by the EDI/C-CDA cases plus these three regressions), a malformed-input case maps to the same 400 "Parse error" shape as every other feature, and both the no-JS form-post success and error paths render into the page. Verified end-to-end with a real browser (Puppeteer, installed to the session scratch dir per this project's own established practice, not a project dependency) before considering this phase done, not just via these route-level tests - see the architecture bullet above for what that pass specifically checked and why a genuinely separate page was chosen partly *because* it sidesteps needing this kind of manual verification for cross-page pane interference.

**MDM provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder.py`) extends the same discipline to MDM, the fourth and final HL7v2 message type: `test_mdm_provenance_recording_does_not_change_bundle_output` is parametrized across all 4 real MDM fixtures for T02 (basic, minimal, obx-with-caret, same-author-authenticator) plus the basic fixture reused across all 5 remaining triggers (T04/T06/T08/T10/T11), the identical byte-for-byte Bundle-output-unchanged proof every earlier slice's own version already established. `test_mdm_t02_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks exact expected entries against `mdm_t02_basic.hl7`'s own full TXA shape - status (hardcoded/inferred, never read from TXA-19's actual value), contentType/type/masterIdentifier/date/securityLabel/description all direct from their own TXA fields, both the author/authenticator display strings and the originator Practitioner's own name (proving the "one field, two destinations" case), and the joined two-OBX-line `Binary.data` fact (with its own `"(×2 segments)"` disclosed location). Three dedicated regression tests cover the real complications the mapper-layer instrumentation bullet above discloses: `test_mdm_t02_same_author_authenticator_records_authenticator_display_from_txa10` (the deduped-Practitioner case's own `authenticator.display` fact must still point at TXA-10, not TXA-9, even though both reference the identical underlying resource), `test_mdm_t02_obx_with_caret_binary_data_is_not_truncated_in_crosswalk` (the crosswalk's own recorded `Binary.data` value must match the untruncated `raw_field_str` text, not a caret-truncated one), and `test_mdm_t02_minimal_fixture_without_pv1_records_no_encounter_facts` (no PV1 segment means `build_minimal_encounter` never runs, so zero Encounter-scoped facts anywhere in the resolved crosswalk, confirmed via the same no-orphan-fact check every earlier slice's own zero-behavior-change test already runs).

**C-CDA provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_cda.py`, a new file - see that file's own docstring for why it's kept separate from `test_provenance_recorder.py` rather than appended, mirroring `test_cda_validation.py`'s own precedent) extends the same discipline to the first non-HL7v2 format: `test_cda_provenance_recording_does_not_change_bundle_output` is parametrized across 16 real fixtures spanning all three document types, including several whose own section (Medications/Allergies/Vitals/Results/Procedures/Immunizations) is deliberately NOT instrumented this slice - direct proof the no-op `recorder=None` additions across those 8 sibling `SECTION_BUILDERS` files don't alter their own output either, not just the two (header, Problems) that do real work. `test_ccd_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks exact expected entries against `ccd_basic.xml`'s own already-known values (the same fixture `test_ccd_mapping.py` itself asserts against). Four dedicated regression tests cover the real complications the mapper-layer instrumentation bullet above discloses, none of which any earlier (HL7v2) slice needed to handle: `test_ccd_header_multiplicities_records_correct_indices_for_repeating_fields` (two `<name>`s/`<addr>`s/`<telecom>`s/encounter `<id>`s - every kept FHIR array index and its XML repetition index must both be correct, including a root-only patient `<id>`'s own `urn:oid:` fallback value still producing a real fact rather than a skipped one), `test_ccd_effective_time_variants_records_bare_value_vs_low_high_shapes` (all three legal `IVL_TS` shapes - bare `@value`, `<low>`-only, `<low>`+`<high>`, `nullFlavor` - each producing the correct, shape-specific `source_location` via the new `effective_time_location` helper, not a guessed default), `test_ccd_problem_status_observation_overrides_act_status_records_nested_location` (the nested Status Observation's own deeper location wins over the Concern Act's own `statusCode` location when both could apply, mirroring `test_ccd_mapping.py`'s identical Bundle-side "resolved value wins" assertion), and `test_ccd_problem_negated_produces_no_condition_facts` (a `negationInd="true"` entry produces zero Condition-scoped facts anywhere in the resolved crosswalk, not just an empty Condition). `test_ccd_medications_section_records_no_facts_yet` is the direct proof for `ccd_medications_basic.xml` specifically that a real `MedicationRequest` still gets built (conversion unaffected) while carrying zero recorded facts, confirming the "accept recorder, no-op" contract holds for a section that hasn't shipped its own slice yet. `test_discharge_summary_hospital_discharge_diagnosis_condition_also_recorded` is the direct regression proof for the "for free" `build_hospital_discharge_diagnoses` extension the mapper-layer bullet discloses - both the discharge-diagnosis Condition (`category="encounter-diagnosis"`) and the plain-Problems Condition in the same `discharge_summary_basic.xml` document get real, independently-resolved `code.coding[0].code` facts. `test_history_and_physical_header_only_fixture_records_header_facts` confirms a document with no Problems section at all (an H&P-specific narrative section this app doesn't recognize instead) still produces correct header-only facts, no crash and no spurious Condition-scoped ones.

**EDI provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_edi.py`, a new file, the same "own file per format" precedent `test_provenance_recorder_cda.py` already established) extends the same discipline to the third and final input format: `test_edi_provenance_recording_does_not_change_bundle_output` is parametrized across all 4 real 270/271 fixtures, all 4 real 276/277 fixtures, plus fixtures from the remaining still-uninstrumented EDI families (278, 835, 837P) - direct proof both the real instrumentation AND the no-op `recorder=None` additions across the other builder files (plus the "free" `assemble_bundle` facts they now get) don't alter any of their own output. `test_edi_270_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks exact expected entries against `edi_270_basic.x12`'s own already-known values (the same fixture `test_eligibility_270_mapping.py` itself asserts against) - payer/subscriber/dependent name and identifier facts, the inferred `status`/`purpose[0]`, the direct `item[0].coding[0].code`/`servicedDate`/`created`, and both `Bundle.identifier`/`.timestamp`. `test_edi_271_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` does the same for both of that fixture's own EB groups, each resolving to its own independently-indexed `insurance[0].item[i]` facts - including the first boolean fact this pillar has ever recorded (`insurance[0].inforce`), confirmed stringified (`"True"`/`"False"`) per `ProvenanceEntry.value`'s own `str | None` type rather than assumed. Three dedicated regression tests cover the real complications the mapper-layer instrumentation bullet above discloses: `test_edi_271_rejected_records_direct_outcome_and_disposition` (a real `AAA01="N"` rejection flips `.outcome`/`.disposition` from their own inferred defaults to direct facts pointing at `AAA-1`/`AAA-3`, while `.inforce` - genuinely independent of the rejection - still resolves to its own inferred `False`), `test_edi_270_no_dependent_records_only_subscriber_facts` (no 2000D loop at all means exactly one Patient's worth of name/birthDate/gender facts, not two, with the standard no-orphan-fact check), and `test_edi_278_non_instrumented_family_still_gets_free_bundle_level_facts`/`test_edi_835_non_instrumented_family_with_no_bht_gets_zero_facts` proving the "free"-facts design itself (every resolved fact for a still-uninstrumented BHT-bearing family is `Bundle.identifier`/`.timestamp`-scoped, nothing resource-specific; 835's own lack of a BHT segment means it doesn't even get those free facts - `entries == []` despite a real, multi-resource Bundle being built).

**EDI 276/277 provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_edi.py`) extends the same discipline to the second EDI family: `test_edi_276_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks both of that fixture's own Tasks (subscriber's and dependent's) resolve `status="requested"`/`intent="order"`/`code.text="Claim Status"` as `derivation="inferred"` with no `businessStatus` fact at all - direct proof 276's own request-side Task never fabricates STC-sourced facts it doesn't have. `test_edi_277_basic_records_direct_status_and_business_status` checks the sibling response fixture's own two STC-populated Tasks resolve `status` as `derivation="direct"` from `STC-1.1`, with both `businessStatus.coding[]` entries pointing at their own correct sub-component locations (`STC-1.1`/`STC-1.2`). `test_edi_277_error_status_records_failed_status` is the dedicated regression proof that `edi_277_error_status.x12`'s own `"E1"` category resolves directly to `Task.status="failed"` via `STC_CATEGORY_PREFIX_TO_TASK_STATUS`'s own `"E"` entry, not the `"completed"`/inferred default a missing-STC case would produce.

**EDI 278 provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_edi.py`) extends the same discipline to the third EDI family: `test_edi_278_request_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks the real request fixture's own inferred `status`/`use`/`type.coding[0].code`/`priority.text`/`item[0].productOrService.text` (no `UM` segment present) alongside its two direct, independently-indexed `diagnosis[]` facts from one `HI` segment's own two composites. `test_edi_278_response_certified_records_direct_outcome_and_item_category` checks the sibling response fixture (whose own `UM` segment IS present, unlike the basic request) resolves `item[0].productOrService` as a direct fact from `UM-3` instead, plus the `ClaimResponse`'s own direct `outcome`/`preAuthRef`/adjudication facts from `HCR` - including a dedicated assertion that `Claim.created` and `ClaimResponse.created` resolve to the *identical* displayed string, the direct regression proof for the `datetime`-vs-string formatting bug the mapper-layer bullet above discloses catching before commit. `test_edi_278_response_denied_records_two_adjudication_entries` confirms both `HCR01`(action)/`HCR03`(reason) codes resolve to their own separately-indexed `adjudication[]` entries when both are present, with no spurious `preAuthRef` fact when `HCR02` is genuinely absent. `test_edi_278_request_with_dependent_records_both_patients` confirms the with-dependent fixture's own two Patients (subscriber and dependent) both carry their own independently-resolved name facts, not just one.

**EDI 835 provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_edi.py`) extends the same discipline to the fourth EDI family: `test_edi_835_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks the real basic fixture's own payer/payee Organization facts (from `N1`), the `PaymentReconciliation`'s own inferred `status` alongside its direct `created`/`paymentDate` (both from the identical `BPR16` source, confirmed to share the same value and location) and `paymentAmount.value` (from `BPR02`), its one `detail[0]` entry's own `type`/`identifier`/`amount` facts (from `CLP2`/`CLP1`/`CLP4`), `Bundle.identifier` (from `TRN02`, this family's only header identifier source), and a dedicated assertion that `Bundle.timestamp` is genuinely absent from the resolved crosswalk - 835's own disclosed permanent gap (no full date+time field exists anywhere in this transaction set), not an oversight. `test_edi_835_multi_claim_records_independently_indexed_details` confirms both of that fixture's own `CLP` groups (one paid, one denied with a `$0.00` `CLP04`) resolve to their own, correctly-indexed `detail[0]`/`detail[1]` facts - including confirming the zero-but-present paid amount is recorded, not silently skipped the way a genuinely absent value would be.

**EDI 837P provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_edi.py`) extends the same discipline to the fifth EDI family, and the first covering a `Claim.item[]` shape: `test_edi_837p_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks the real basic fixture's own two service lines' procedure code/charge/diagnosis-pointers/care-team-sequence/serviced-date facts, both diagnoses, and the claim's own inferred `status`/`type`/`use`/`priority` alongside its direct `identifier`/`total`/`created` - including a dedicated assertion that `item[0]`'s own `diagnosisSequence` fact resolves to the joined string `"1,2"` (both pointers) while `item[1]`'s resolves to `"1"` (a single pointer), and that both items' own `locationCodeableConcept` facts independently point at the identical claim-wide `CLM-5.1` source. `test_edi_837p_with_dependent_records_both_patients` confirms the with-dependent fixture's own two Patients (subscriber and dependent) both carry their own independently-resolved name facts, and - since that fixture's own claim has no rendering provider at all - that no `careTeam` fact is recorded, proving the inferred `role` fact is genuinely conditional on the provider's presence, not unconditionally emitted.

**EDI 837I provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder_edi.py`) extends the same discipline to the sixth EDI family, and the dedicated regression proof for the `segment_repetition` fix: `test_edi_837i_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks the real basic fixture's own three diagnoses - resolving `diagnosis[0]` (from the Principal `HI` segment) and `diagnosis[1]`/`diagnosis[2]` (from the Other `HI` segment) to three genuinely distinct `edi_location("HI", ..., segment_repetition=...)` source locations, with a dedicated assertion that `diagnosis[0]`'s and `diagnosis[1]`'s own locations are NOT equal despite both resolving from intra-segment position 1 - plus the claim's own inferred `type="institutional"`, the discharge-status `supportingInfo[0]` entry's own inferred `category`/direct `code` (from `CL1-3`), the first service line's own direct `productOrService`/`revenue` facts (from `SV2-2.2`/`SV2-1`), and the inferred `careTeam[0].role`. `test_edi_837i_with_dependent_records_text_fallback_for_procedure_less_line` is the dedicated regression proof for the new three-way `productOrService` branch 837I's own service lines need that 837P's don't: the with-dependent fixture's own single line has no procedure composite, only a revenue code, so `productOrService.text` must resolve as a *direct* fact (`"Revenue code 0120"`, built from the real `SV2-1` value) rather than the fully-synthetic `"Unspecified service"` an *inferred* fallback would produce - confirming the crosswalk correctly distinguishes "a real value, just reformatted" from "no real value at all," the same distinction this pillar's own established `derivation` field exists to make.

**ORU provenance testing** (`tests/test_provenance_recorder.py`) extends the same discipline to ORU: `test_oru_provenance_recording_does_not_change_bundle_output` is parametrized across all 8 real ORU fixtures spanning all 5 triggers (R01's own basic/minimal/shared-performer/caret-in-free-text edge cases, plus one basic fixture each for R30/R31/R32/R40), the identical byte-for-byte Bundle-output-unchanged proof every earlier slice's own version already established. `test_oru_r01_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` checks exact expected entries against `oru_r01_basic.hl7`'s own two-report, three-observation, one-performer shape - both reports' own independently-resolved status/code/effectiveDateTime/issued facts (proving the per-loop-iteration id-hoisting doesn't cross-contaminate which report a fact belongs to), the WBC observation's own valueQuantity.value/.unit/referenceRange/interpretation/effectiveDateTime facts, and the OBX-16 performer's own identifier/name facts. Three dedicated regression tests cover the real complications the mapper-layer instrumentation bullet above discloses: `test_oru_r01_shared_performer_recorded_once_not_once_per_observation` (a cache-hit Practitioner must not produce a second, redundant `name[0].family` fact), `test_oru_r01_free_text_value_with_caret_is_not_truncated_in_crosswalk` (the crosswalk's own recorded `valueString` must match the untruncated `raw_field_str` value, not a caret-truncated one), and `test_oru_minimal_fixture_without_pv1_records_no_encounter_facts` (no PV1 segment means `build_minimal_encounter` never runs, so zero Encounter-scoped facts should appear anywhere in the resolved crosswalk - confirmed via the same `len(entries) == len(recorder.facts)` no-orphan-fact check every earlier slice's own zero-behavior-change test already runs).


**SIU provenance testing** (appended to `tests/test_provenance_recorder.py`, not a new file - both formats share the identical deterministic-uuid zero-behavior-change harness) mirrors ADT's own shape at the same depth: `test_siu_provenance_recording_does_not_change_bundle_output` parametrized across 13 real fixtures spanning all 6 triggers (including the AIG-as-Location, AIP-id-only, multiple-NTE, partial-TQ1, SCH-11-fallback, and stale-SCH-9-duration edge-case fixtures the forward mapper's own test suite already established) - the same byte-for-byte `model_dump(exclude_none=True)` comparison proving instrumentation changes nothing. `test_siu_s12_basic_crosswalk_matches_known_field_values` is the one test this slice needed that ADT's own equivalent didn't: a direct assertion that AIP-3 produces *two* independent entries (the materialized `Practitioner.name[0].family` and the `Appointment.participant[1].actor.display` string) sharing the identical `source_location`, proving the "one source field, two FHIR destinations" case is actually captured, not just designed for. `test_siu_s12_sch11_fallback_timing_points_at_sch_not_tq1`/`test_siu_s13_duration_prefers_tq1_over_stale_sch9` are the dedicated regression tests for `_timing_source_location`/`_tq1_duration_used`'s own dual-source resolution, mirroring `test_adt_a13_with_discharge_period_start_points_at_pv1_44_not_evn2`'s exact role for ADT's analogous PV1-44-or-EVN-2 case. `test_siu_s12_aig_device_records_type_and_identifier` confirms the AIG-derived `Device`'s own `type`/`identifier` facts resolve correctly against that specific resource's own `Bundle.entry[N]` index, not the Appointment's.
